00218: def descendants(obj):
00219:     if obj is None:
00220:         return []
00221:     result=[]
00222:     for child in obj.children:
00223:         if child.type=='MESH':
00224:             result.append(child)
00225:         result.extend(descendants(child))
00226:     return result
00227: 
00228: def assign_group(name,material):
00229:     for ob in descendants(bpy.data.objects.get(name)):
00230:         ob.data.materials.clear()
00231:         ob.data.materials.append(material)

00233: def textile(name,patterned=False):
00234:     mat=bpy.data.materials.new(name)
00235:     mat.use_nodes=True
00236:     n=mat.node_tree.nodes
00237:     l=mat.node_tree.links
00238:     bs=n.get('Principled BSDF')
00239:     bs.inputs['Base Color'].default_value=(.76,.68,.55,1)
00240:     bs.inputs['Roughness'].default_value=.86
00241:     geo=n.new('ShaderNodeNewGeometry')
00242:     noise=n.new('ShaderNodeTexNoise')
00243:     noise.inputs['Scale'].default_value=240
00244:     noise.inputs['Detail'].default_value=2
00245:     l.new(geo.outputs['Position'],noise.inputs['Vector'])
00246:     bump=n.new('ShaderNodeBump')
00247:     bump.inputs['Strength'].default_value=.16
00248:     bump.inputs['Distance'].default_value=.002
00249:     l.new(noise.outputs['Fac'],bump.inputs['Height'])
00250:     l.new(bump.outputs['Normal'],bs.inputs['Normal'])
00251:     if patterned:
00252:         wy=n.new('ShaderNodeTexWave')
00253:         wy.wave_type='BANDS'
00254:         wy.bands_direction='Y'
00255:         wy.inputs['Scale'].default_value=10
00256:         l.new(geo.outputs['Position'],wy.inputs['Vector'])
00257:         wx=n.new('ShaderNodeTexWave')
00258:         wx.wave_type='BANDS'
00259:         wx.bands_direction='X'
00260:         wx.inputs['Scale'].default_value=13
00261:         l.new(geo.outputs['Position'],wx.inputs['Vector'])
00262:         ty=n.new('ShaderNodeMath')
00263:         ty.operation='GREATER_THAN'
00264:         ty.inputs[1].default_value=.94
00265:         l.new(wy.outputs['Fac'],ty.inputs[0])
00266:         tx=n.new('ShaderNodeMath')
00267:         tx.operation='GREATER_THAN'
00268:         tx.inputs[1].default_value=.78
00269:         l.new(wx.outputs['Fac'],tx.inputs[0])
00270:         mul=n.new('ShaderNodeMath')
00271:         mul.operation='MULTIPLY'
00272:         l.new(tx.outputs[0],mul.inputs[0])
00273:         l.new(ty.outputs[0],mul.inputs[1])
00274:         mix=n.new('ShaderNodeMixRGB')
00275:         mix.inputs[1].default_value=(.78,.71,.61,1)
00276:         mix.inputs[2].default_value=(.43,.20,.19,1)
00277:         l.new(mul.outputs[0],mix.inputs[0])
00278:         l.new(mix.outputs[0],bs.inputs['Base Color'])
00279:     return mat

00191: 
00192: def group_object(name):
00193:     return bpy.data.objects.get(name)
00194: 
00195: def descendants(obj):
00196:     if obj is None:
00197:         return []
00198:     out=[]
00199:     for child in obj.children:
00200:         if child.type=='MESH':
00201:             out.append(child)
00202:         out.extend(descendants(child))
00203:     return out
00204: 
00205: def fabric_material(name, patterned=True):
00206:     mat=bpy.data.materials.new(name)
00207:     mat.use_nodes=True
00208:     nd=mat.node_tree.nodes
00209:     lk=mat.node_tree.links
00210:     bs=nd.get('Principled BSDF')
00211:     bs.inputs['Roughness'].default_value=.87
00212:     coord=nd.new('ShaderNodeTexCoord')
00213:     sep=nd.new('ShaderNodeSeparateXYZ')
00214:     lk.new(coord.outputs['Object'],sep.inputs[0])
00215:     noise=nd.new('ShaderNodeTexNoise')
00216:     noise.inputs['Scale'].default_value=180
00217:     noise.inputs['Roughness'].default_value=.8
00218:     lk.new(coord.outputs['Object'],noise.inputs['Vector'])
00219:     bump=nd.new('ShaderNodeBump')
00220:     bump.inputs['Strength'].default_value=.20
00221:     bump.inputs['Distance'].default_value=.008
00222:     lk.new(noise.outputs['Fac'],bump.inputs['Height'])
00223:     lk.new(bump.outputs[0],bs.inputs['Normal'])
00224:     if patterned:
00225:         wave=nd.new('ShaderNodeTexWave')
00226:         wave.wave_type='BANDS'
00227:         wave.bands_direction='Y'
00228:         wave.inputs['Scale'].default_value=19
00229:         wave.inputs['Distortion'].default_value=.12
00230:         lk.new(coord.outputs['Object'],wave.inputs['Vector'])
00231:         ramp=nd.new('ShaderNodeValToRGB')
00232:         ramp.color_ramp.elements[0].position=.80
00233:         ramp.color_ramp.elements[0].color=(.75,.685,.57,1)
00234:         ramp.color_ramp.elements[1].position=.88
00235:         ramp.color_ramp.elements[1].color=(.48,.29,.27,1)
00236:         extra=ramp.color_ramp.elements.new(.96)
00237:         extra.color=(.79,.73,.62,1)
00238:         lk.new(wave.outputs['Color'],ramp.inputs[0])
00239:         lk.new(ramp.outputs[0],bs.inputs['Base Color'])
00240:     else:
00241:         bs.inputs['Base Color'].default_value=(.80,.74,.60,1)
00242:     return mat
00243:

00245: plain=fabric_material('Soft ivory pillow weave',False)
00246: for obj in descendants(group_object('pillow')):
00247:     obj.data.materials.clear()

00250: # Editable upholstered panels, attached to their meaningful chaise parent.
00251: def panel(name,coords,material):
00252:     mesh=bpy.data.meshes.new(name+' mesh')
00253:     mesh.from_pydata(coords,[],[list(range(len(coords)))])
00254:     mesh.update()
00255:     ob=bpy.data.objects.new(name,mesh)
00256:     bpy.context.collection.objects.link(ob)
00257:     ob.parent=group_object('chaise')
00258:     ob.data.materials.append(material)
00259:     solid=ob.modifiers.new('Upholstery thickness','SOLIDIFY')
00260:     solid.thickness=.035
00261:     bevel=ob.modifiers.new('Soft panel edges','BEVEL')
00262:     bevel.width=.025
00263:     bevel.segments=3
00264:     return ob

00284: # Slight grain and soft sheen on the individual parquet meshes.
00285: woodmat=bpy.data.materials.new('Walnut grain')
00286: woodmat.use_nodes=True
00287: nodes=woodmat.node_tree.nodes
00288: links=woodmat.node_tree.links
00289: bs=nodes.get('Principled BSDF')
00290: bs.inputs['Roughness'].default_value=.38
00291: tc=nodes.new('ShaderNodeTexCoord')
00292: mapping=nodes.new('ShaderNodeVectorMath')
00293: mapping.operation='MULTIPLY'
00294: mapping.inputs[1].default_value=(3,85,7)
00295: links.new(tc.outputs['Generated'],mapping.inputs[0])
00296: noise=nodes.new('ShaderNodeTexNoise')
00297: noise.inputs['Scale'].default_value=3
00298: links.new(mapping.outputs[0],noise.inputs['Vector'])
00299: bump=nodes.new('ShaderNodeBump')
00300: bump.inputs['Strength'].default_value=.13
00301: bump.inputs['Distance'].default_value=.012
00302: links.new(noise.outputs['Fac'],bump.inputs['Height'])
00303: links.new(bump.outputs[0],bs.inputs['Normal'])
00304: for ob in descendants(group_object('parquet')):
00305:     old=ob.data.materials[0]
00306:     mat=woodmat.copy()
00307:     mat.node_tree.nodes.get('Principled BSDF').inputs['Base Color'].default_value=old.diffuse_color
00308:     ob.data.materials.clear()
00309:     ob.data.materials.append(mat)
